Tool result content is text/image only; details is an application-defined required value, usage and addedToolNames are optional, and terminate is not a message role or persistent event. A consumer must not treat arbitrary custom data or tool output as policy. There is no event retention, serial number, size cap or persistence in these types; those belong to higher layers.",

Its shallow context copy retains the original messages array, so raw continuation appends can mutate the caller array; Agent avoids that by supplying its own snapshot.\n\nThe sink may return void or a promise and every explicit await is an ordering/backpressure point. Raw run functions return newly added messages; no session file or event journal is written. agentLoop/agentLoopContinue adapt the sink to EventStream, complete its result when agent_end arrives and end it after the run promise resolves.
